+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

+    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}

+    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)

+            sizeof(int) * 2;

+            static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds)), memory_capacity));

+    std::vector<int> materialize(int seed,
+                                 const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
+                                 double threshold) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered_, clustered.data(),
+                             clustered.size() * sizeof(unsigned char),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying clustered mask");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds_, &seed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying winning seed");
+        buildCandidateClusters<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            d_points_, d_clustered_, d_seeds_, 1, n_, threshold,
+            d_members_, d_in_cluster_, d_cardinalities_);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "launching winning candidate kernel");
+        int count = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(&count, d_cardinalities_, sizeof(int),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning cardinality");
+        std::vector<int> members(count);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(members.data(), d_members_,
+                             members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning members");
+        return members;
+    }
+
+  private:
+    int n_;
+    size_t capacity_ = 0;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_in_cluster_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

+static inline double pointDistance(const Point& a, const Point& b) {
+    const double dx = a.x - b.x;
+    const double dy = a.y - b.y;
+    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+

+class CudaClusterEngine {
+  public:
+    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)
+        : n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, points.size() * sizeof(Point)),
+                  "allocating device points");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, points.size() * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating device state");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), points.size() * sizeof(Point),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying points to device");
+
+        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), "querying GPU memory");
+        (void)total_bytes;
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed =
+            static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char)) +
+            sizeof(int) * 2;
+        // Keep ample room for the CUDA runtime and other ranks sharing a GPU.
+        const size_t memory_capacity = (free_bytes / 2) / std::max<size_t>(1, bytes_per_seed);
+        capacity_ = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(
+            static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds)), memory_capacity));
+
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device seeds");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device cardinalities");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating candidate members");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_in_cluster_,
+                             capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating candidate masks");
     }
-

-    if (cluster_members) {
-        *cluster_members = members;
-    }

-    return static_cast<int>(members.size());

+    std::vector<int> materialize(int seed,

+                  "copying winning seed");

+            d_points_, d_clustered_, d_seeds_, 1, n_, threshold,
+            d_members_, d_in_cluster_, d_cardinalities_);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "launching winning candidate kernel");
+        int count = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(&count, d_cardinalities_, sizeof(int),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),

+        std::vector<int> members(count);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(members.data(), d_members_,
+                             members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning members");

+  private:
+    int n_;

+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_in_cluster_ = nullptr;

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

+}

+    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)

+            sizeof(int) * 2;

+            static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds)), memory_capacity));

+                             clustered.size() * sizeof(unsigned char),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying clustered mask");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds_, &seed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying winning seed");
+        buildCandidateClusters<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            d_points_, d_clustered_, d_seeds_, 1, n_, threshold,
+            d_members_, d_in_cluster_, d_cardinalities_);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "launching winning candidate kernel");
+        int count = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(&count, d_cardinalities_, sizeof(int),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning cardinality");
+        std::vector<int> members(count);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(members.data(), d_members_,
+                             members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning members");
+        return members;
+    }
+
+  private:
+    int n_;
+    size_t capacity_ = 0;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_in_cluster_ = nullptr;
+};
+
+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

+    const double dx = a.x - b.x;
+    const double dy = a.y - b.y;
+    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}

+class CudaClusterEngine {
+  public:
+    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)
+        : n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, points.size() * sizeof(Point)),
+                  "allocating device points");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, points.size() * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating device state");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), points.size() * sizeof(Point),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying points to device");
+
+        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), "querying GPU memory");
+        (void)total_bytes;
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed =
+            static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char)) +
+            sizeof(int) * 2;
+        // Keep ample room for the CUDA runtime and other ranks sharing a GPU.
+        const size_t memory_capacity = (free_bytes / 2) / std::max<size_t>(1, bytes_per_seed);
+        capacity_ = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(
+            static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds)), memory_capacity));
+
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device seeds");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device cardinalities");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating candidate members");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_in_cluster_,
+                             capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating candidate masks");
     }
-

-    }

+    std::vector<int> materialize(int seed,

+                  "copying winning seed");

+            d_points_, d_clustered_, d_seeds_, 1, n_, threshold,
+            d_members_, d_in_cluster_, d_cardinalities_);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "launching winning candidate kernel");
+        int count = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(&count, d_cardinalities_, sizeof(int),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning cardinality");
+        std::vector<int> members(count);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(members.data(), d_members_,
+                             members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning members");

+  private:
+    int n_;
+    size_t capacity_ = 0;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_in_cluster_ = nullptr;

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

+    const double dx = a.x - b.x;
+    const double dy = a.y - b.y;
+    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}

+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                       int seed_count, int n, double threshold,
+                                       int* __restrict__ all_members,
+                                       unsigned char* __restrict__ all_in_cluster,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int candidate_cluster = blockIdx.x;
+    if (candidate_cluster >= seed_count) return;
+
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+    unsigned char* in_cluster =
+        all_in_cluster + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+
+    __shared__ double best_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int best_point[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int selected;
+
+    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += blockDim.x) in_cluster[i] = 0;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const int seed = seeds[candidate_cluster];
+        members[0] = seed;
+        in_cluster[seed] = 1;
+        member_count = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (true) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_point = -1;
+        const int count = member_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_distance = 0.0;
+            const Point p = points[candidate];
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const Point q = points[members[j]];
+                const double dx = p.x - q.x;
+                const double dy = p.y - q.y;
+                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+                if (d > max_distance) max_distance = d;
+            }
+            if (max_distance < threshold &&
+                (max_distance < thread_best_distance ||
+                 (max_distance == thread_best_distance &&
+                  candidate < thread_best_point))) {
+                thread_best_distance = max_distance;
+                thread_best_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_distance[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_distance;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_point;
+        __syncthreads();

+  public:
+    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)
+        : n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, points.size() * sizeof(Point)),
+                  "allocating device points");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, points.size() * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating device state");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), points.size() * sizeof(Point),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),

+        cudaCheck(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), "querying GPU memory");
+        (void)total_bytes;

+            static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char)) +
+            sizeof(int) * 2;

+        const size_t memory_capacity = (free_bytes / 2) / std::max<size_t>(1, bytes_per_seed);

+            static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds)), memory_capacity));

+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating candidate members");

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

-// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
-inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
-    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
-    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
-    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                       int seed_count, int n, double threshold,
+                                       int* __restrict__ all_members,
+                                       unsigned char* __restrict__ all_in_cluster,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int candidate_cluster = blockIdx.x;
+    if (candidate_cluster >= seed_count) return;
+
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+    unsigned char* in_cluster =
+        all_in_cluster + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+
+    __shared__ double best_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int best_point[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int selected;
+
+    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += blockDim.x) in_cluster[i] = 0;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const int seed = seeds[candidate_cluster];
+        members[0] = seed;
+        in_cluster[seed] = 1;
+        member_count = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (true) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_point = -1;
+        const int count = member_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_distance = 0.0;
+            const Point p = points[candidate];
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const Point q = points[members[j]];
+                const double dx = p.x - q.x;
+                const double dy = p.y - q.y;
+                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+                if (d > max_distance) max_distance = d;
+            }
+            if (max_distance < threshold &&
+                (max_distance < thread_best_distance ||
+                 (max_distance == thread_best_distance &&
+                  candidate < thread_best_point))) {
+                thread_best_distance = max_distance;
+                thread_best_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_distance[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_distance;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_point;
+        __syncthreads();

+                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const double other_distance = best_distance[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int this_point = best_point[threadIdx.x];
+                const double this_distance = best_distance[threadIdx.x];

+                    (this_point < 0 || other_distance < this_distance ||
+                     (other_distance == this_distance && other_point < this_point))) {
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                    best_distance[threadIdx.x] = other_distance;

+            selected = best_point[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                in_cluster[selected] = 1;
+                members[member_count++] = selected;
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (selected < 0) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
+

+class CudaClusterEngine {
+  public:
+    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)
+        : n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, points.size() * sizeof(Point)),
+                  "allocating device points");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, points.size() * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating device state");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), points.size() * sizeof(Point),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying points to device");
+
+        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), "querying GPU memory");
+        (void)total_bytes;
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed =
+            static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char)) +
+            sizeof(int) * 2;
+        // Keep ample room for the CUDA runtime and other ranks sharing a GPU.
+        const size_t memory_capacity = (free_bytes / 2) / std::max<size_t>(1, bytes_per_seed);
+        capacity_ = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(
+            static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds)), memory_capacity));
+
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device seeds");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device cardinalities");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating candidate members");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_in_cluster_,
+                             capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating candidate masks");
     }

+    std::vector<int> materialize(int seed,
+                                 const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,

+            d_points_, d_clustered_, d_seeds_, 1, n_, threshold,
+            d_members_, d_in_cluster_, d_cardinalities_);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "launching winning candidate kernel");
+        int count = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(&count, d_cardinalities_, sizeof(int),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning cardinality");
+        std::vector<int> members(count);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(members.data(), d_members_,
+                             members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning members");

+  private:
+    int n_;
+    size_t capacity_ = 0;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_in_cluster_ = nullptr;

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

+static inline double pointDistance(const Point& a, const Point& b) {
+    const double dx = a.x - b.x;
+    const double dy = a.y - b.y;
+    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}

+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                       int seed_count, int n, double threshold,
+                                       int* __restrict__ all_members,
+                                       unsigned char* __restrict__ all_in_cluster,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int candidate_cluster = blockIdx.x;
+    if (candidate_cluster >= seed_count) return;
+
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+    unsigned char* in_cluster =
+        all_in_cluster + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+
+    __shared__ double best_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int best_point[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int selected;
+
+    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += blockDim.x) in_cluster[i] = 0;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const int seed = seeds[candidate_cluster];
+        members[0] = seed;
+        in_cluster[seed] = 1;
+        member_count = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (true) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_point = -1;
+        const int count = member_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_distance = 0.0;
+            const Point p = points[candidate];
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const Point q = points[members[j]];
+                const double dx = p.x - q.x;
+                const double dy = p.y - q.y;
+                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+                if (d > max_distance) max_distance = d;
+            }
+            if (max_distance < threshold &&
+                (max_distance < thread_best_distance ||
+                 (max_distance == thread_best_distance &&
+                  candidate < thread_best_point))) {
+                thread_best_distance = max_distance;
+                thread_best_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_distance[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_distance;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_point;

+class CudaClusterEngine {
+  public:
+    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)
+        : n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, points.size() * sizeof(Point)),
+                  "allocating device points");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, points.size() * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating device state");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), points.size() * sizeof(Point),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying points to device");
+
+        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), "querying GPU memory");
+        (void)total_bytes;
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed =
+            static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char)) +
+            sizeof(int) * 2;
+        // Keep ample room for the CUDA runtime and other ranks sharing a GPU.
+        const size_t memory_capacity = (free_bytes / 2) / std::max<size_t>(1, bytes_per_seed);
+        capacity_ = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(
+            static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds)), memory_capacity));
+
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device seeds");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device cardinalities");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating candidate members");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_in_cluster_,
+                             capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(unsigned char)),

+    std::vector<int> materialize(int seed,
+                                 const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
+                                 double threshold) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered_, clustered.data(),
+                             clustered.size() * sizeof(unsigned char),

+                  "copying winning seed");
+        buildCandidateClusters<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            d_points_, d_clustered_, d_seeds_, 1, n_, threshold,
+            d_members_, d_in_cluster_, d_cardinalities_);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "launching winning candidate kernel");
+        int count = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(&count, d_cardinalities_, sizeof(int),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning cardinality");
+        std::vector<int> members(count);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(members.data(), d_members_,
+                             members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning members");
+        return members;
+    }
+
+  private:
+    int n_;
+    size_t capacity_ = 0;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_in_cluster_ = nullptr;
+};

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

-// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
-inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {

+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                       int seed_count, int n, double threshold,
+                                       int* __restrict__ all_members,
+                                       unsigned char* __restrict__ all_in_cluster,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int candidate_cluster = blockIdx.x;
+    if (candidate_cluster >= seed_count) return;
+
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+    unsigned char* in_cluster =
+        all_in_cluster + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+
+    __shared__ double best_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int best_point[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int selected;
+
+    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += blockDim.x) in_cluster[i] = 0;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const int seed = seeds[candidate_cluster];
+        members[0] = seed;
+        in_cluster[seed] = 1;
+        member_count = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (true) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_point = -1;
+        const int count = member_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_distance = 0.0;
+            const Point p = points[candidate];
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const Point q = points[members[j]];
+                const double dx = p.x - q.x;
+                const double dy = p.y - q.y;
+                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+                if (d > max_distance) max_distance = d;
+            }
+            if (max_distance < threshold &&
+                (max_distance < thread_best_distance ||
+                 (max_distance == thread_best_distance &&
+                  candidate < thread_best_point))) {
+                thread_best_distance = max_distance;
+                thread_best_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_distance[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_distance;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_point;
+        __syncthreads();

+        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {

+                const double other_distance = best_distance[threadIdx.x + offset];
+                const int this_point = best_point[threadIdx.x];
+                const double this_distance = best_distance[threadIdx.x];
+                if (other_point >= 0 &&
+                    (this_point < 0 || other_distance < this_distance ||
+                     (other_distance == this_distance && other_point < this_point))) {
+                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
+                    best_distance[threadIdx.x] = other_distance;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-

-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            selected = best_point[0];
+            if (selected >= 0) {
+                in_cluster[selected] = 1;
+                members[member_count++] = selected;
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (selected < 0) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
+

 }

+class CudaClusterEngine {
+  public:
+    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)
+        : n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, points.size() * sizeof(Point)),
+                  "allocating device points");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, points.size() * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating device state");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), points.size() * sizeof(Point),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying points to device");
+
+        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), "querying GPU memory");
+        (void)total_bytes;
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed =
+            static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char)) +
+            sizeof(int) * 2;
+        // Keep ample room for the CUDA runtime and other ranks sharing a GPU.
+        const size_t memory_capacity = (free_bytes / 2) / std::max<size_t>(1, bytes_per_seed);
+        capacity_ = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(
+            static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds)), memory_capacity));
+
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device seeds");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device cardinalities");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating candidate members");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_in_cluster_,
+                             capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating candidate masks");
     }

+    std::vector<int> materialize(int seed,
+                                 const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,

+                  "copying winning seed");

+            d_points_, d_clustered_, d_seeds_, 1, n_, threshold,
+            d_members_, d_in_cluster_, d_cardinalities_);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "launching winning candidate kernel");
+        int count = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(&count, d_cardinalities_, sizeof(int),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning cardinality");
+        std::vector<int> members(count);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(members.data(), d_members_,
+                             members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning members");
+        return members;

+  private:
+    int n_;
+    size_t capacity_ = 0;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_in_cluster_ = nullptr;

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }
 
-// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
+static inline double pointDistance(const Point& a, const Point& b) {
+    const double dx = a.x - b.x;
+    const double dy = a.y - b.y;
+    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}

+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                       int seed_count, int n, double threshold,
+                                       int* __restrict__ all_members,
+                                       unsigned char* __restrict__ all_in_cluster,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int candidate_cluster = blockIdx.x;
+    if (candidate_cluster >= seed_count) return;
+
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+    unsigned char* in_cluster =
+        all_in_cluster + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;

+    __shared__ double best_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int best_point[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int selected;

+    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += blockDim.x) in_cluster[i] = 0;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const int seed = seeds[candidate_cluster];
+        members[0] = seed;

+

+            if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_distance = 0.0;
+            const Point p = points[candidate];
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const Point q = points[members[j]];
+                const double dx = p.x - q.x;
+                const double dy = p.y - q.y;
+                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+                if (d > max_distance) max_distance = d;
+            }

+            }
+        }

+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_point;

+class CudaClusterEngine {
+  public:
+    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)
+        : n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, points.size() * sizeof(Point)),
+                  "allocating device points");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, points.size() * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating device state");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), points.size() * sizeof(Point),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying points to device");
+
+        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), "querying GPU memory");
+        (void)total_bytes;
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed =
+            static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char)) +
+            sizeof(int) * 2;
+        // Keep ample room for the CUDA runtime and other ranks sharing a GPU.
+        const size_t memory_capacity = (free_bytes / 2) / std::max<size_t>(1, bytes_per_seed);
+        capacity_ = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(
+            static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds)), memory_capacity));
+
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device seeds");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device cardinalities");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating candidate members");

+    std::vector<int> materialize(int seed,

+            d_points_, d_clustered_, d_seeds_, 1, n_, threshold,
+            d_members_, d_in_cluster_, d_cardinalities_);

+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning cardinality");
+        std::vector<int> members(count);

+                             members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning members");

+  private:
+    int n_;
+    size_t capacity_ = 0;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_in_cluster_ = nullptr;

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-

    70	__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
    71	                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
    72	                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,
    73	                                       int seed_count, int n, double threshold,
    74	                                       int* __restrict__ all_members,
    75	                                       unsigned char* __restrict__ all_in_cluster,
    76	                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
    77	    const int candidate_cluster = blockIdx.x;
    78	    if (candidate_cluster >= seed_count) return;
    79	
    80	    int* members = all_members + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
    81	    unsigned char* in_cluster =
    82	        all_in_cluster + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
    83	
    84	    __shared__ double best_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
    85	    __shared__ int best_point[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
    86	    __shared__ int member_count;
    87	    __shared__ int selected;
    88	
    89	    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += blockDim.x) in_cluster[i] = 0;
    90	    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
    91	        const int seed = seeds[candidate_cluster];
    92	        members[0] = seed;
    93	        in_cluster[seed] = 1;
    94	        member_count = 1;
    95	    }
    96	    __syncthreads();
    97	
    98	    while (true) {
    99	        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
   100	        int thread_best_point = -1;
   101	        const int count = member_count;
   102	
   103	        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n;
   104	             candidate += blockDim.x) {
   105	            if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
   106	
   107	            double max_distance = 0.0;
   108	            const Point p = points[candidate];
   109	            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
   110	                const Point q = points[members[j]];
   111	                const double dx = p.x - q.x;
   112	                const double dy = p.y - q.y;
   113	                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
   114	                if (d > max_distance) max_distance = d;
   115	            }
   116	            if (max_distance < threshold &&
   117	                (max_distance < thread_best_distance ||
   118	                 (max_distance == thread_best_distance &&
   119	                  candidate < thread_best_point))) {
   120	                thread_best_distance = max_distance;
   121	                thread_best_point = candidate;
   122	            }
   123	        }
   124	
   125	        best_distance[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_distance;
   126	        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_point;
   127	        __syncthreads();
   128	
   129	        // Reduce a total ordering of (diameter, point index).  The index is the
   130	        // deterministic tie break used by the sequential reference.
   131	        for (int offset = blockDim.x / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
   132	            if (threadIdx.x < offset) {
   133	                const int other_point = best_point[threadIdx.x + offset];
   134	                const double other_distance = best_distance[threadIdx.x + offset];
   135	                const int this_point = best_point[threadIdx.x];
   136	                const double this_distance = best_distance[threadIdx.x];
   137	                if (other_point >= 0 &&
   138	                    (this_point < 0 || other_distance < this_distance ||
   139	                     (other_distance == this_distance && other_point < this_point))) {
   140	                    best_point[threadIdx.x] = other_point;
   141	                    best_distance[threadIdx.x] = other_distance;
   142	                }
   143	            }
   144	            __syncthreads();
   145	        }
   146	
   147	        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
   148	            selected = best_point[0];
   149	            if (selected >= 0) {
   150	                in_cluster[selected] = 1;
   151	                members[member_count++] = selected;
   152	            }
   153	        }
   154	        __syncthreads();
   155	        if (selected < 0) break;
   156	    }
   157	
   158	    if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[candidate_cluster] = member_count;
   159	}

   161	class CudaClusterEngine {
   162	  public:
   163	    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)
   164	        : n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
   165	        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, points.size() * sizeof(Point)),
   166	                  "allocating device points");
   167	        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, points.size() * sizeof(unsigned char)),
   168	                  "allocating device state");
   169	        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), points.size() * sizeof(Point),
   170	                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
   171	                  "copying points to device");
   172	
   173	        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
   174	        cudaCheck(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), "querying GPU memory");
   175	        (void)total_bytes;
   176	        const size_t bytes_per_seed =
   177	            static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char)) +
   178	            sizeof(int) * 2;
   179	        // Keep ample room for the CUDA runtime and other ranks sharing a GPU.
   180	        const size_t memory_capacity = (free_bytes / 2) / std::max<size_t>(1, bytes_per_seed);
   280	
   281	std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
   282	                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
   283	    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
   284	    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
   285	    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
   286	    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
   287	    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
   288	    int remaining = n;
   289	
   290	    while (remaining > 0) {
   291	        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
   292	        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
   293	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   294	        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
   295	            eligible[seed] =
   296	                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
   297	        }
   298	        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
   299	        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
   300	        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
   301	            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
   302	
   303	        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
   304	        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
   305	        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
   306	            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
   307	            : 0;
   308	        long long global_key = 0;
   309	        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
   310	                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   311	        if (global_key == 0) break;
   312	
   313	        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
   314	        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
   315	        std::vector<int> members;
   316	        if (rank == owner)
   317	            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
   318	        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
   319	        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   320	        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
   321	        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   322	
   323	        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
   324	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   325	        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
   326	        remaining -= member_count;
   327	    }
   328	    return clusters;
   329	}
   330	
   331	static inline double pointDistance(const Point& a, const Point& b) {
   332	    const double dx = a.x - b.x;
   333	    const double dy = a.y - b.y;
   334	    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
   335	}
   385	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   386	}

+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                       int seed_count, int n, double threshold,
+                                       int* __restrict__ all_members,
+                                       unsigned char* __restrict__ all_in_cluster,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int candidate_cluster = blockIdx.x;
+    if (candidate_cluster >= seed_count) return;
+
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+    unsigned char* in_cluster =
+        all_in_cluster + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+
+    __shared__ double best_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int best_point[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int selected;
+
+    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += blockDim.x) in_cluster[i] = 0;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const int seed = seeds[candidate_cluster];
+        members[0] = seed;
+        in_cluster[seed] = 1;
+        member_count = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (true) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_point = -1;
+        const int count = member_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_distance = 0.0;
+            const Point p = points[candidate];
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const Point q = points[members[j]];
+                const double dx = p.x - q.x;
+                const double dy = p.y - q.y;
+                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+                if (d > max_distance) max_distance = d;
+            }
+            if (max_distance < threshold &&
+                (max_distance < thread_best_distance ||
+                 (max_distance == thread_best_distance &&
+                  candidate < thread_best_point))) {
+                thread_best_distance = max_distance;
+                thread_best_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_distance[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_distance;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_point;
+        __syncthreads();

+class CudaClusterEngine {
+  public:
+    CudaClusterEngine(const std::vector<Point>& points, int maximum_local_seeds)
+        : n_(static_cast<int>(points.size())) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_points_, points.size() * sizeof(Point)),
+                  "allocating device points");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_clustered_, points.size() * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating device state");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_points_, points.data(), points.size() * sizeof(Point),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying points to device");
+
+        size_t free_bytes = 0, total_bytes = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemGetInfo(&free_bytes, &total_bytes), "querying GPU memory");
+        (void)total_bytes;
+        const size_t bytes_per_seed =
+            static_cast<size_t>(n_) * (sizeof(int) + sizeof(unsigned char)) +
+            sizeof(int) * 2;
+        // Keep ample room for the CUDA runtime and other ranks sharing a GPU.
+        const size_t memory_capacity = (free_bytes / 2) / std::max<size_t>(1, bytes_per_seed);
+        capacity_ = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min<size_t>(
+            static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, maximum_local_seeds)), memory_capacity));
+
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_seeds_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device seeds");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_cardinalities_, capacity_ * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating device cardinalities");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_members_, capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(int)),
+                  "allocating candidate members");
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&d_in_cluster_,
+                             capacity_ * static_cast<size_t>(n_) * sizeof(unsigned char)),
+                  "allocating candidate masks");

+    std::vector<int> materialize(int seed,
+                                 const std::vector<unsigned char>& clustered,
+                                 double threshold) {
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_clustered_, clustered.data(),
+                             clustered.size() * sizeof(unsigned char),
+                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),

+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(d_seeds_, &seed, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
+                  "copying winning seed");
+        buildCandidateClusters<<<1, CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE>>>(
+            d_points_, d_clustered_, d_seeds_, 1, n_, threshold,
+            d_members_, d_in_cluster_, d_cardinalities_);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "launching winning candidate kernel");
+        int count = 0;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(&count, d_cardinalities_, sizeof(int),
+                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning cardinality");
+        std::vector<int> members(count);
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(members.data(), d_members_,
+                             members.size() * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost),
+                  "reading winning members");
+        return members;
+    }
+
+  private:
+    int n_;
+    size_t capacity_ = 0;
+    Point* d_points_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_clustered_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_seeds_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_cardinalities_ = nullptr;
+    int* d_members_ = nullptr;
+    unsigned char* d_in_cluster_ = nullptr;
+};

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }
 
-// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
+static inline double pointDistance(const Point& a, const Point& b) {
+    const double dx = a.x - b.x;
+    const double dy = a.y - b.y;
+    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+}
+
 bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
-                     const std::vector<Point>& points,

codex
Implemented the hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA version in-place.

Key changes:

- CUDA evaluates candidate clusters concurrently, one block per seed.
- MPI distributes seeds across ranks and deterministically selects the global winner.
- OpenMP parallelizes host-side state updates and validation.
- GPU memory-aware batching supports larger datasets.
- MPI ranks select node-local GPUs automatically.
- Deterministic tie-breaking preserves sequential semantics.
- Updated [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/qtclustering_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r1/qtclustering/CMakeLists.txt) to require MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA.
- Executable remains `qtclustering`; no files were added.

Validation completed successfully:

- Clean CMake/CUDA build.
- 1-rank and 2-rank runs produced identical hashes.
- Default-size test: 1,000 points, 4 MPI ranks, 4 OpenMP threads/rank.
- All 1,000 points clustered; validation passed.
- Result hash: `dbef3881b0a560cd`.

-// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
-inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {

+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* __restrict__ points,
+                                       const unsigned char* __restrict__ clustered,
+                                       const int* __restrict__ seeds,
+                                       int seed_count, int n, double threshold,
+                                       int* __restrict__ all_members,
+                                       unsigned char* __restrict__ all_in_cluster,
+                                       int* __restrict__ cardinalities) {
+    const int candidate_cluster = blockIdx.x;
+    if (candidate_cluster >= seed_count) return;
+
+    int* members = all_members + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+    unsigned char* in_cluster =
+        all_in_cluster + static_cast<size_t>(candidate_cluster) * n;
+
+    __shared__ double best_distance[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int best_point[CUDA_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    __shared__ int member_count;
+    __shared__ int selected;
+
+    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += blockDim.x) in_cluster[i] = 0;
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        const int seed = seeds[candidate_cluster];
+        members[0] = seed;
+        in_cluster[seed] = 1;
+        member_count = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    while (true) {
+        double thread_best_distance = DBL_MAX;
+        int thread_best_point = -1;
+        const int count = member_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < n;
+             candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_distance = 0.0;
+            const Point p = points[candidate];
+            for (int j = 0; j < count; ++j) {
+                const Point q = points[members[j]];
+                const double dx = p.x - q.x;
+                const double dy = p.y - q.y;
+                const double d = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
+                if (d > max_distance) max_distance = d;
+            }
+            if (max_distance < threshold &&
+                (max_distance < thread_best_distance ||
+                 (max_distance == thread_best_distance &&
+                  candidate < thread_best_point))) {
+                thread_best_distance = max_distance;
+                thread_best_point = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_distance[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_distance;
+        best_point[threadIdx.x] = thread_best_point;
+        __syncthreads();

+            sizeof(int) * 2;

+                  "allocating device cardinalities");

+std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
+                                  double threshold, int rank, int world_size) {
+    const int n = static_cast<int>(points.size());
+    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered(n, 0);
+    std::vector<unsigned char> eligible(n, 0);
+    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
+    CudaClusterEngine engine(points, (n + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+    int remaining = n;
+
+    while (remaining > 0) {
+        // OpenMP constructs the rank-local work mask; the compacting pass is
+        // ordered so runs remain bit-for-bit deterministic.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed) {
+            eligible[seed] =
+                (!clustered[seed] && seed % world_size == rank) ? 1 : 0;
         }
+        std::vector<int> local_seeds;
+        local_seeds.reserve((remaining + world_size - 1) / world_size);
+        for (int seed = 0; seed < n; ++seed)
+            if (eligible[seed]) local_seeds.push_back(seed);
+
+        const auto local = engine.bestLocalCandidate(local_seeds, clustered, threshold);
+        const long long base = static_cast<long long>(n) + 1;
+        const long long local_key = local.first > 0
+            ? static_cast<long long>(local.first) * base + (n - local.second)
+            : 0;
+        long long global_key = 0;
+        MPI_Allreduce(&local_key, &global_key, 1, MPI_LONG_LONG, MPI_MAX,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (global_key == 0) break;
+
+        const int best_seed = n - static_cast<int>(global_key % base);
+        const int owner = best_seed % world_size;
+        std::vector<int> members;
+        if (rank == owner)
+            members = engine.materialize(best_seed, clustered, threshold);
+        int member_count = rank == owner ? static_cast<int>(members.size()) : 0;
+        MPI_Bcast(&member_count, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank != owner) members.resize(member_count);
+        MPI_Bcast(members.data(), member_count, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+        clusters.push_back({members, best_seed});
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+        for (int i = 0; i < member_count; ++i) clustered[members[i]] = 1;
+        remaining -= member_count;
     }
-    
     return clusters;
 }

+    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

tokens used
60,223
Implemented the hybrid MPI/OpenMP/CUDA version in-place.

Key changes:

- CUDA evaluates candidate clusters concurrently, one block per seed.
- MPI distributes seeds across ranks and deterministically selects the global winner.
- OpenMP parallelizes host-side state updates and validation.
- GPU memory-aware batching supports larger datasets.
